I. 
Introduction 
1 
Q. 
Please introduce the members of the Revenue Requirements Panel. 
2 
A. 
The Panel consists of James M. Molloy, David B. Doxsee, and Stephanie A. 
3 
Briggs. 
4 
 
5 
Q.  
Is this the same Panel that submitted testimony previously in these 
6 
proceedings? 
7 
A. 
Yes.  The terms defined in the Panel’s direct testimony have the same 
8 
definitions here.   
9 
 
10 
Q. 
What is the purpose of the Panel’s corrections and updates testimony? 
11 
A. 
The Panel’s corrections and updates testimony explains and supports certain 
12 
corrections and/or updates to KEDNY’s January 29, 2016 base rate filing in 
13 
this proceeding.  The Panel sponsors the following exhibits, all of which were 
14 
prepared by or under the supervision and direction of one or more members of 
15 
the Panel and which, in all cases, refer to KEDNY: 
16 
 
Exhibit ___ (RRP-1CU) Statement of Operating Income; 
17 
 
Exhibit ___ (RRP-3CU) O&M Expenses by Expense Type; 
18 
 
Exhibit ___ (RRP-4CU) Depreciation Expense; 
19 
 
Exhibit ___ (RRP-5CU) Taxes Other than Income Taxes; 
20 
 
Exhibit ___ (RRP-6CU) Federal and State Income Taxes;

5 
Q. 
Please explain how the corrections and/or updates are organized. 
6 
A. 
The Company began with the exhibits as originally filed on January 29, 2016.  
7 
The exhibits submitted in this filing show corrections and/or updates and 
8 
indicate the change from the original filing.  The exhibits are labeled with 
9 
“CU” after the original label.  For example, Exhibit __ (RRP-1) in the January 
10 
29, 2016 filing is the Statement of Operating Income.  The exhibit showing 
11 
the corrections and/or updates to the Statement of Operating Income is labeled 
12 
Exhibit ___ (RRP-1CU).  The first page of each exhibit provides a summary 
13 
of all adjustments made and reflected in that exhibit.  This summary, or lead 
14 
page, is identified as “Corrections and Updates” under each exhibit number.  
15 
The revised schedules and pages supporting each exhibit correspond to the 
16 
schedules and pages originally filed.  The Company has only included 
17 
exhibits, summaries, schedules, and pages where corrections and/or updates 
18 
were made.  The information requests referenced in the Panel’s testimony are 
19 
included in the workpapers in Exhibit ___ (RRP-11CU) and correspond to the 
20 
specific exhibits and schedules where the corrections and/or updates were 
21 
4


--- Page 7 ---
 
Corrections and Updates Testimony of The Revenue Requirements Panel 
Page 3 of 23 
 
made.  For example, if a correction and/or update is shown in Exhibit ___ 
1 
(RRP-3CU), Schedule 2 based on an information request, that information 
2 
request is included in the workpapers supporting Exhibit ___ (RRP-3CU), 
3 
Schedule 2.  Where the attachments to information requests are voluminous, 
4 
the Company included only the written response and relevant attachments.   
5 
 
6 
Q. 
Has the Company changed the format of any exhibits in the corrections 
7 
and updates filing? 
8 
A. 
Yes.  The Company has revised the format of Exhibit ___ (RRP-1CU) to more 
9 
clearly illustrate the effect of the proposal to move SIR costs from recovery 
10 
through the SIR surcharge to recovery in base rates.  This revision is in 
11 
accordance with the response to Information Request DPS-212 (RC-1).     
12 
 
13 
Q. 
What impact do the corrections and/or updates have on the revenue 
14 
requirements in this proceeding? 
15 
A. 
The corrections and/or updates decrease the Company’s net revenue 
16 
requirement for the Rate Year from $244.953 million to $244.699 million. 
17 
This represents the net impact of a proposed base rate increase of $289.990 
18 
million and the proposed reduction in the SIR surcharge of $45.291 million. 
19 
 
20 
5


--- Page 8 ---
 
Corrections and Updates Testimony of The Revenue Requirements Panel 
Page 4 of 23 
 
As explained in the January 29, 2016 filing, to mitigate the impact of a 
1 
significant rate increase on customers, the Company presented a potential 
2 
three-year revenue increase phase-in that could be considered as part of a 
3 
settlement with Department of Public Service Staff and other parties.  The 
4 
Company continues to support the concept of a multi-year agreement as a 
5 
means of mitigating the impact of the requested base rate increase on 
6 
customers.   
7 
 
8 
Q. 
What are some of the adjustments the Panel describes in its corrections 
9 
and updates testimony?     
10 
A. 
Adjustments the Panel describes include corrections and/or updates in Service 
11 
Company rent expense, changes to labor expense, and updates and/or 
12 
corrections for transportation costs.  The Company also presents adjustments 
13 
for changes in anticipated operating expenses for certain initiatives and 
14 
updated rate case expense.  This filing also presents changes in the 
15 
Company’s State income tax expense, as well as changes in taxes other than 
16 
income taxes.  The Company is updating its plant balance, accumulated 
17 
deferred income tax balance and other minor rate base item balances based on 
18 
actuals as of December 31, 2015, as well as incorporating updated capital 
19 
forecasts from January 1, 2016 through the Rate Year and the Data Years.   
20 
 
21 
6


--- Page 9 ---
 
Corrections and Updates Testimony of The Revenue Requirements Panel 
Page 5 of 23 
 
The Company also is proposing to update its cost and level of debt, which 
1 
reduces the overall rate of return as described in the corrections and updates 
2 
testimony of Stephen H. Caldwell.  The updated overall rate of return is 
3 
incorporated in Exhibit ___ (RRP-1CU).    
4 
 
5 
Finally, the Company is updating its inflation projections, which will reduce 
6 
the proposed revenue requirement in the case.  The updated inflation 
7 
projections affect costs broadly and result in the need to update most of the 
8 
Company’s revenue requirements schedules.      
9

III.

Q. 
Please explain Exhibit ___ (RRP-3CU). 
2

3 
O&M expenses for the Rate Year and Data Years.  The exhibit consists of a 
4 
lead Corrections and Updates page, a Summary showing total departmental 
5 
O&M expenses in the Historic Test Year, Rate Year, and Data Years, and 33 
6 
schedules.  The schedules are numbered to correspond to the schedules 
7 
submitted in the Company’s January 29, 2016 filing.  For example, in the 
8 
January 29, 2016 filing, Exhibit ___ (RRP-3), Schedule 4, showed the costs 
9 
associated with employee expenses.  The corrections and/or updates to 
10 
employee expenses are reflected in Exhibit ___ (RRP-3CU), Schedule 4.   
11 
 
12 
Q. 
Please explain the expense specific schedules in Exhibit ___ (RRP-3CU). 
13 
A. 
The expense specific schedules that reflect corrections and/or updates to the 
14 
January 29, 2016 filing are discussed below.  To the extent that the updated 
15 
inflation projection is the only reason for a change in a schedule, the revised 
16 
schedule is not separately discussed below, but is included in the revised 
17 
schedules in this filing.  A list of expense specific schedules that are impacted 
18 
only by inflation is provided following the Panel’s testimony.

Schedule 4 – Employee Expenses 
1 
Schedule 4 reflects three adjustments to address mapping issues between 
2 
Exhibit ___ (RRP-2) and Exhibit ___ (RRP-3) in the January 29, 2016 filing 
3 
that resulted in certain normalizing adjustments to employee expenses in the 
4 
Historic Test Year being incorrectly shown.  The adjustments were made to 
5 
Historic Test Year employee expense in accordance with Information Request 
6 
DPS-257 (SW-12).  The revised schedule also reflects the effects of the 
7 
updated inflation factor.   
8 
 
 
9 
 
Schedule 9 – Service Company Rent Expense 
10 
Schedule 9 shows four adjustments to Service Company rent expense.   
11 
 
12 
The first adjustment relates to the anticipated in-service date of KEDNY’s 
13 
planned Sustainability Hub at the Metrotech office in Brooklyn.  The 
14 
Company originally assumed an in-service date of December 31, 2016.  The 
15 
Company now anticipates that the Sustainability Hub will be in-service mid-
16 
year of the Rate Year.  Exhibit ___ (RRP-3CU), Schedule 9, reflects this 
17 
correction.  This correction was made in accordance with Information Request 
18 
DPS-241 (MF-2). 
19 
 
20 
9


--- Page 12 ---
 
Corrections and Updates Testimony of The Revenue Requirements Panel 
Page 8 of 23 
 
The second adjustment is to remove costs from the Rate Year and Data 
1 
Years to reflect KEDNY’s share of a $0.521 million total adjustment for 
2 
both KEDNY and KEDLI related to implementation of the Companies’ Gas 
3 
Transportation Information System (“GTIS”).  The adjustment represents the 
4 
AFUDC amount for KEDNY and KEDLI for the period from the initial 
5 
target GTIS implementation date of November 1, 2014 through the 
6 
anticipated implementation date of April 30, 2016.  As indicated in the 
7 
Panel’s direct testimony (pages 51-52), the Company inadvertently omitted 
8 
the adjustment in the initial revenue requirement.  
9 
 
10 
The third and fourth adjustments correct for the incorrect listing of the Hub 
11 
Drive Security Enhancement project as an IS project in the initial filing rather 
12 
than as a Facilities project.  These adjustments were made in accordance with 
13 
Information Request DPS-225 (MEW-8).   
14 
 
15 
 
Schedule 23 – Labor 
16 
Schedule 23 shows four changes that modify labor expense from what was 
17 
included in the initial filing. 
18 
 
19 
The first adjustment corrects how SIR labor costs were represented in the 
20 
initial filing.  As described in the Panel’s direct testimony (page 73), the 
21 
10


--- Page 13 ---
 
Corrections and Updates Testimony of The Revenue Requirements Panel 
Page 9 of 23 
 
Company is proposing to recover in base rates the costs of certain SIR 
1 
employees whose costs have historically been deferred.  However, in making 
2 
the adjustment to add deferred Service Company labor costs charged to SIR 
3 
during the Historic Test Year to Historic Test Year labor expense for purposes 
4 
of forecasting Rate Year and Data Years labor expense, the Company 
5 
inadvertently added the costs to KEDNY’s labor expense rather than to the 
6 
Service Company’s labor expense.   
7 
 
8 
Q. 
Please describe the second adjustment to labor expense. 
9 
A. 
The second adjustment relates to the inadvertent omission from the initial 
10 
filing of costs associated with labor from the National Grid Engineering 
11 
Services LLC (Engineering Service Company).  The correction includes these 
12 
costs in the calculation of total labor costs in the Historic Test Year 
13 
accounting used to allocate total Service Company labor to KEDNY.  This 
14 
results in increases of 106 FTE management positions and 208 FTE 
15 
represented positions to the employee count.  Adjustments were also made to 
16 
remove management and represented FTEs of the Engineering Service 
17 
Company on long-term leave that are not expected to return.   
18 
 
19 
Q. 
Please describe the third adjustment to labor expense. 
20

A. 
The Company is removing a temporary adjustment reflected in Exhibit ___ 
1

2 
2.  As explained in the response to IR DPS-210 (LJQ-9), due to an issue when 
3

4

5

6

11

12 
the 12 months ending June 30, 2017.  This updated projection revises the 
13 
projected salary increase rate used in the January 29 filing for KEDNY 
14 
management salaries from 3.0% to 3.2%, and for Service Company 
15 
management salaries from 3.5% to 3.2%.

employees were incorrectly excluded from the original filings for both KEDLI

VI. 
Federal and State Income Taxes 
4 
Q. 
Please describe any additional corrections and/or updates made to the 
5 
Company’s forecast of federal and state income tax expense. 
6 
A. 
The New York Department of Taxation and Finance adopted emergency 
7 
corporate franchise tax regulations regarding the Metropolitan Transportation 
8 
Authority (“MTA”) surcharge imposed under Tax Law Article 9-A.  Effective 
9 
January 1, 2016 the MTA surcharge increased from 25.6% to 28% of the NYS 
10 
tax imposed.  The rate will remain the same in any succeeding tax year, unless 
11 
a new rate is determined.  Therefore, the MTA rate was updated from the 
12 
Historic Test Year to reflect the increased amount for the Rate Year and Data 
13 
Years. 
14 
 
 
15

Q.

schedules showing the adjustments to net utility plant (Schedule 1), regulatory

A. 
Forecast of Net Utility Plant In Service 
6 
Q. 
Please describe the changes made to the forecast of average net utility 
7 
plant, as shown in Exhibit ___ (RRP-7CU), Schedule 1.   
8 
A. 
Schedule 1 reflects three adjustments.  The first adjustment reflects updates to 
9 
the Company’s net utility plant forecasts.  The Company updated the forecasts 
10 
to use the actual December 31, 2015 CWIP, plant in service, depreciation 
11 
reserve, and RWIP beginning balances.   
12 
 
13 
The second adjustment to the plant forecast includes updates to reflect 
14 
schedule and/or budget changes in the Company’s capital plan, in addition to 
15 
revised in-service dates for certain major projects, as discussed in the 
16 
corrections and updates testimony of the GIOP.  The updated balances as of 
17 
December 31, 2015, updated capital plan, and revised in-service dates have a 
18 
corresponding impact on depreciation expense, property taxes, and deferred 
19 
taxes, as reflected in Exhibit ___ (RRP-4CU), Exhibit ___ (RRP-5CU), 
20 
Schedule 1, and Exhibit ___ (RRP-7CU), Schedule 3, respectively.

Corrections and Updates Testimony of The Revenue Requirements Panel

1 
Q. 
Please describe the third adjustment to the forecast of net utility plant. 
2 
A. 
The third adjustment was to correct the sign and beginning balance of RWIP 
3 
in the net utility plant forecast presented in Exhibit __ (RRP-11), Workpapers 
4 
to Exhibit RRP-7, Schedule 1, Workpaper 1.  The beginning RWIP balance 
5 
was inadvertently included in the net plant forecast with a reversed sign, and 
6 
also was not fully allocated to all forecast projects.  Both these RWIP 
7 
corrections have been incorporated into the net plant forecast in Exhibit ___ 
8 
(RRP-11CU), Workpapers to RRP-7CU, Schedule 1, Workpaper 1.   
9 
 
10 
 
B. 
Deferred Taxes 
11 
Q. 
Please describe the changes made to the deferred tax forecasts, as shown 
12 
in Exhibit ___ (RRP-7CU), Schedule 3. 
13 
A. 
Schedule 3 reflects four adjustments to federal and state ADIT for the Rate 
14 
Year and Data Years, as well as a flow through adjustment for the MTA 
15 
surcharge change discussed in Section VI, above.   
16 
 
17 
The first adjustment updates ADIT balances for both KEDNY and KEDLI in 
18 
accordance with the response to IR DPS-231 (JPC-7), Supplemental 2.   
19 
 
20 
20


--- Page 23 ---
 
Corrections and Updates Testimony of The Revenue Requirements Panel 
Page 19 of 23 
 
The next adjustment updates the estimated ADIT balances for October, 
1 
November, and December 2015 to rate base ADIT actuals on the general 
2 
ledger.  This includes updates to estimates in the initial filing for federal ADIT 
3 
associated with bonus depreciation for the period January 2015 – December 
4 
2015 to actuals, as well as updates to the initial estimate of the federal return 
5 
to provision related to rate base.  Estimates for these items were included in 
6 
the January 29, 2016 filing as out-of-period adjustments related to the fiscal 
7 
year ended March 31, 2015.  These adjustments were subsequently recorded 
8 
in the general ledger in December 2015 and thus included in the updated 
9 
actual deferred tax balances at December 2015. 
10 
 
11 
The next adjustment relates to updated state ADIT.  The January 29, 2016 
12 
filing did not include an updated estimate of the state ADIT balance because 
13 
not all the state income tax returns were finalized at the time of filing.  In 
14 
February 2016, the Company recorded in the general ledger the state return to 
15 
provision out of period adjustment related to the fiscal year ended March 31, 
16 
2015.  The updated state ADIT balance was subsequently calculated and 
17 
recorded and is now included in the ADIT balances in this corrections and 
18 
updates filing.   
19 
 
20 
21


--- Page 24 ---
 
Corrections and Updates Testimony of The Revenue Requirements Panel 
Page 20 of 23 
 
The next adjustment corrects the rate base treatment of FAS 109 amortization.  
1 
The Company has amortized FAS 109 costs reflected as a regulatory liability 
2 
on the Company’s books into the tax calculation to provide customers the 
3 
benefit of the expense; however, it inadvertently failed to make a 
4 
corresponding adjustment to rate base.   
5 
 
6 
 
These updates are shown in Exhibit ___ (RRP-7CU), Schedule 3.
